As-Is/To-Be Mermaid 다이어그램과 심각도별 진단을 내고, **승인받으면 개선 코드까지 적용**한다. 단일 화면 내부의 사용성·접근성 점검(폼 라벨·터치 크기·대비·한글)은 ux-reviewer 담당이고, 시각 브랜드는 web-design-auditor 담당이므로 겹치지 않는다. ([kyjyjn-code/eca](https://github.com/kyjyjn-code/eca/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/interaction-flow-architect.md))
- **interaction-intuiter**: Intuits each interactive-by-design element's action and endpoint, with a confidence and a question per low-confidence item. Dispatched by the Lead per codebase in Phase -1B after route-mapper. Writes only INTERACTION_INTUITION_MAP.md. ([paulingram/claude-skills](https://github.com/paulingram/claude-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/interaction-intuiter.md) · ★1)
- **interaction-lifecycle-checklist**: Use this for every new or changed mode, panel, sheet, popover or async load (#410). Audits kept reopening work over combinations the brief and test matrix never named: Esc while a panel is open, focus after a mode change, refresh during a load, closing a window with work in flight, relaunch, and results that land after the user moved on. This file names those cases once so briefs, test matrices an ([anzchy/mado](https://github.com/anzchy/mado/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/interaction-lifecycle-checklist.md) · ★1)
- **interaction-loading-detective**: Use before shipping changes that touch onboarding, modal/sheet rendering, async loading states, or first-time-only UI cards. Catches the bug class where a modal appears at the wrong moment (e.g., onboarding showing AFTER sign-in instead of skipping for returning users), a spinner that never resolves, a transition that lands in a broken intermediate state, or a "first-time-only" card that has no permanent reach (should also live in profile drawer + cover kebab). ([tangjennii-wq/skinetude](https://github.com/tangjennii-wq/skinetude/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/interaction-loading-detective.md))
- **interaction-mapper**: Atomic pairwise interaction probe — input ≥2 POE entities (item × item, support × skill, unique × passive notable, ascendancy × unique, jewel × cluster, flask × buff, curse × monster res), output verdict "X có work với Y không, work thế nào, exclusion nào chặn, math impact bao nhiêu". Lighter workflow 3 phases — skip ultragoal (single decision). Standalone mode viết content/guides/&lt;slug&gt;.md qua /write-mechanic-tutorial. Subroutine mode (invoked từ parent agent) return compact summary, không persist file. ([nguyenvanduocit/orb-of-ai](https://github.com/nguyenvanduocit/orb-of-ai/blob/HEAD/template/.claude/agents/interaction-mapper.md) · 2 copies)

- **interaction-review**: Reviews a change for side effects on other features: a shared object that the change writes or reads, and a second feature that depends on it. It uses the map in docs/feature-interactions.md. Use it before a commit on any change under Sources/ or to a page-wiring Public/*.js file. CI does not run it, so the session that makes the change does. Read-only: it reports, it does not edit. ([JimWallace/Chickadee](https://github.com/JimWallace/Chickadee/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/interaction-review.md))
